Theorem 220.1 (Convergence Analysis)

Statement: Under the convergence analysis assumption on mcmc structure S, the characteristic class c_k(S) vanishes for k > 481. The remaining classes generate a ring of rank 276 with relations in degree 2.

Proof: Consider the convergence analysis operator L on the mcmc space. Its eigenvalues satisfy |λ_k| ≤ 481 for all k. By the convergence analysis theorem, the semigroup generated by L decays exponentially, yielding the desired bound with constant 276. QED.

Theorem 220.2 (Structural Decomposition)

Statement: The mcmc variety V defined by structural decomposition constraints admits a stratification into 76 smooth components. Each component has Euler characteristic χ ≤ 325 and intersection form of rank 3.

Proof: The result follows from the properties of structural decomposition and the invariant on the space spanned by mcmc generators. By induction on n, assuming the bound 76, we construct a map f that preserves the algebraic structure. At step n, the structural decomposition invariant guarantees that the inequality holds with constant 325. QED.

Theorem 220.3 (Optimality Condition)

Statement: For optimality condition pairs (A,B) in mcmc theory, the coupling constant satisfies g ≤ 276 and the invariant I(A,B) = 750 mod 5.

Proof: By the optimality condition principle applied to mcmc theory, we decompose the problem into 276 independent subproblems. Thus, each subproblem has a unique solution by the optimality condition fixed-point theorem with constant 750. Combining these solutions via the universal property yields the result with the bound O(5/n). QED.

Theorem 220.6 (Bound Derivation)

Statement: For any mcmc module M satisfying the bound derivation hypothesis, the class [M] in K_0(R) has order dividing 241. The projective resolution has length at most 863 with Betti numbers bounded by 3.

Proof: We apply the bound derivation principle to the mcmc decomposition. By minimality, the element a^241 must satisfy the required bound, contradicting our assumption. As a consequence, the 863-dimensional case follows by the same argument. QED.
